# HMS centralized documentation inventory

Discovered on 28 October 2026. Current branch: `DEV-GENERAL`; HEAD `7df97a7dc8ce7f01abfbcaeec8502bb390c213b5`.

This inventory tracks two categories of documentation separately:
1. **Original source documents (376 active copies)**: Unmodified copies from repository history, comprising 4 original FSD specifications (3 Cross-Module HMS, 1 RBAC), 227 Design documents, 3 Test Case Scenarios, and 142 Test Result documents.
2. **Generated module-specific FSD drafts (45 drafts)**: Standardized functional specification drafts generated for each of the 45 HMS modules under `HMS Project/FSD/<Module>/<Module>_FSD.md`. All generated drafts are explicitly marked `Draft — Pending Review` and ground 359 unique functional requirements (FR-*) traced to source specifications or implementation observations.

Placeholders: 76 `.gitkeep` files remain in empty folders (43 placeholders were removed from FSD folders upon draft generation).

Each row records one source path/blob representation and every ref containing it. Ref-tip commit hashes are recorded in BRANCH_DOCUMENT_MATRIX.md. Working-tree sources are not attributed to branch history. Source approval is not inferred from naming, commit date, or a claim that implementation is complete.
